- Finding set:
  - `R14-WINPARTIAL1-n2 — resolved, high: ReplaceFileW and mutable hard-link recovery are removed.`
  - `R14-WINPARTIAL1-n3 — resolved, high: Windows cleanup opens and identity-checks the pathname, then uses handle disposition.`
  - `R14-WINPARTIAL1-n4 — resolved, high: after first destination identity proof, every path reaches exact-handle flush and second verification, including a reported rename error; all causes remain under completed classification.`
  - `R14-WINPARTIAL1-n5 — resolved, high: source identity is checked on the retained handle and post-open substitution cannot redirect publication.`
  - `R14-WINPARTIAL1-n6 — resolved, medium: the NTFS-only hard-link dependency is removed and explicit unsupported-class fallback uses the same held handle.`
  - `R14-WINTEST1 — resolved, medium: contradictory recovery-path tests are removed and replacement seams now match production behavior.`
  - `R14-WINRESULT1 — resolved, high: first identity proof irrevocably establishes completion; later flush failure or second verification mismatch/error remains ReplacementCompleted, while failures before proof remain non-completed.`
  - `R15-GITIDENT1 — resolved by Reviewer pending hosted acceptance, high: NewGitRepository sets repository-local identity; hostile-global/raw-direct-command regression passes without global mutation.`
  - `R16-WINRENAME1 — resolved by Reviewer pending hosted acceptance, high: production creates and renames relative leaves through one retained type-validated directory authority.`
  - `R16-WINMODE1 — resolved by Reviewer pending hosted acceptance, high: retained create handle has required access/options and applies final requested mode before exact-handle flush.`
  - `R16-WINIDENT1 — resolved by Reviewer pending hosted acceptance, high: production retains the authoritative create handle through publication with no stat/close/reopen source gap.`
  - `R16-WINCLEANUP1 — resolved by Reviewer pending hosted acceptance, medium: handle disposition is allowed only for a proven original temp generation; substitutions and ambiguity are preserved.`
  - `R16-WINRUNNER1 — resolved by final Reviewer pending native hosted acceptance, high: Windows creates the leader suspended with explicit standard-handle inheritance, assigns it to a private non-inherited KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE Job before resume, and closes retained Job authority before output drain on leader exit/cancellation. Failure is pre-execution and nested/restricted Jobs fail closed; native production tests cover immediate child, fast leader, cancellation/control, nested Jobs, launch failure, output/exit/timing.`
  - `A17-WINRESULT2 — resolved pending Reviewer/native acceptance, high: cleanup is disabled before rename; a failed first destination proof permits disposition only after independent proof that the retained handle still names the original temp leaf, otherwise close preserves ambiguous state. Proven publication remains irreversible through mode/flush/second verification with joined completed causes.`
  - `A18-WINPRODTEST1 — resolved pending Reviewer/native acceptance, high: direct Windows production-route tests cover retained relative authority, create access/share/options, mode-before-flush ordering, error-with-effect, first/second verification failures, definitely-unpublished cleanup, and substituted-temp preservation.`
  - `R17-WINNTRENAME1-n1 — unresolved, high: the preferred NtSetInformationFile request incorrectly uses native FileRenameInformation class 10 while supplying replace-plus-POSIX flags. Class 10 interprets only basic ReplaceIfExists semantics; FILE_RENAME_POSIX_SEMANTICS requires FileRenameInformationEx class 65. Use class 65 for the preferred replace-plus-POSIX request, then fall back only on the specified unsupported statuses to class 10 with basic replacement, and update protocol/fallback tests to assert the distinct classes. The current class-10 path can fail with a retained destination handle and therefore does not establish the required retained-generation behavior.`
  - `R18-WINLEGACYTEST1 — resolved, medium: exactly the two obsolete stat/close/reopen assertions were retired while injected callback compatibility and direct production coverage for substitution, destination proof, cleanup preservation, definite non-publication, error-with-effect, requested modes, retained readers, and both verification boundaries remain.`

Windows job 100536231634 failed in 19m57s: Git fixture identity and the suspended-Job runner passed natively, while production atomic publication failed before destination proof. The complete R17/R18 escalation continuation is present only as uncommitted Windows-only worktree changes. Independent host fsutil normal/race, Windows amd64/386 compile-only, and scoped whitespace checks pass. Five unrelated paths and all other shared worktree changes remain excluded and preserved.`
